Every ratio is baseline ÷ measured, so higher is always better: 2.00× means twice as fast, or half the area, or half the memory. Geomean covers LEC-proven, hand-written Pyrope only. norm marks a netlist whose behavioural registers yosys mapped, so it could be counted and timed exactly like the others. STA gate keeps a complete synthesis measurement visible when LiveHD OpenTimer disagrees with OpenSTA; it is excluded from the geomean and detailed in STA accuracy below.

Every ratio is baseline ÷ measured, so higher is always better: 2.00× means twice as fast, or half the area, or half the memory. Geomean covers LEC-proven, hand-written Pyrope only. norm marks a netlist whose behavioural registers yosys mapped, so it could be counted and timed exactly like the others. STA gate keeps a complete synthesis measurement visible when LiveHD OpenTimer disagrees with OpenSTA; it is excluded from the geomean and detailed in STA accuracy below.

STA accuracy — LiveHD OpenTimer vs OpenSTA

A diagnostic on the tables above, not a result in itself: this is how a delay that cannot be trusted gets found. Same netlist, same Liberty, same SDC — only the timer differs. A verdict is not boolean: proven and refuted are answers; timeout, inconclusive, unsupported and error are the absence of one. Until a test is proven, its QoR numbers above are reported but never aggregated — a Pyrope win might be a different circuit.

Equivalence — sources vs synthesized netlist

Two obligations over the same flat, mapped standard-cell design: LiveHD checks Pyrope vs netlist; both the Yosys-backed engine and LiveHD/cvc5 check Verilog vs netlist. Pyrope/cvc5: 316 proven, 16 unsupported, 2 timeout. Verilog/Yosys: 234 proven, 83 inconclusive, 17 timeout. Verilog/cvc5: 310 not-measured, 24 proven. 10 skipped. On the 14 row(s) where both Verilog-vs-netlist engines reached the same verdict, cvc5 is 11.45× the speed of the Yosys-backed one. A timeout exhausted the budget; an inconclusive result returned earlier without either a proof or a counterexample. The table keeps all three checks explicit.
